# Data homes -- where the remaining globals and .rodata belong (generated)

Generated by `platforms/tools/inventory.py`. Each row is an engine data object: a global,
cvar, table, shader text, vtable or typeinfo. The transcribed code **references** it, the original
`libkotor2.so` **exports** it, and no TU in `src/` defines it yet. These are exactly the
zero-filled data stubs the host smoke build has to invent. Most of them are also why a
`extern` in `src/` has no definition anywhere.

Total: **1432 objects, 126077 bytes** in the original.

| evidence | rows | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| class home+stabs | 515 | a vtable/typeinfo/static member: the TU defining most of the class's methods, which is also one of the stabs file's TUs |
| class home | 15 | as above, where the stabs disagree or say nothing (our tree keeps one class per TU; the original file may have held several) |
| stabs+bracket | 1 | the macOS stabs name the file AND both ARM64 neighbours already live in that TU |
| stabs | 343 | the macOS stabs name the original file, and that file maps to exactly one of our TUs |
| stabs+window | 59 | the stabs file is split across several of our TUs; exactly one of them lies between the ARM64 neighbours in link order |
| stabs+user | 238 | the stabs file is split across several of our TUs; exactly one of them references the object |
| stabs (split file, majority) | 37 | the stabs file is split and nothing narrows it: the TU holding most of that file's functions (check the votes) |
| bracket (inferred) | 67 | as bracket, where a neighbour is itself a stabs-placed row from this list |
| stabs-file | 5 | stabs name a file whose functions are not in our tree yet: the TU does not exist |
| link-order window | 64 | neighbours are two different TUs; exactly one referencing TU has its code between them in link order |
| bracket (unexported) | 15 | a name our tree coined for data the original does not export; placed by its address (from the declaration comment) between two neighbours of one TU |
| unexported: declaring TU | 26 | as above, but the neighbours disagree or no address is recorded: the declaring TU is the best guess |
| single user | 32 | no layout evidence; only one TU references it (weak) |
| none | 15 | unresolved: neighbours disagree and no stabs |

How to use: put the definition in the verdict TU, in ARM64 address order among that TU's own
data. The address column is the original's. Initializers come from `tools/rodata.py`
(`.rodata`/`.data`), and a `.bss` object is zero-initialized. "votes" is how many of the mac
file's functions our tree already defines in that TU. `lo`/`hi` are the nearest data symbols
below/above that OUR tree defines, with their TU.
